	focus = {
		id = TUR_african_holdings
		icon = africa

		x = 0
		y = 6

		will_lead_to_war_with = SUD

				type = annex_everything

			set_temp_variable = { wargoal_type = 2 }
			add_threat_from_wargoal_effect = yes
			create_wargoal = {
				type = annex_everything
				target = LBA
			}
			set_temp_variable = { wargoal_on = SUD }
			set_temp_variable = { wargoal_type = 2 }
			add_threat_from_wargoal_effect = yes
			create_wargoal = {
				type = annex_everything
				target = SUD
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = TUR_GREY_WOLVES_AI_FOCUS

					has_global_flag = TUR_TURKISH_LEFT_AI_FOCUS
					has_global_flag = TUR_HIZMET_AI_FOCUS
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					EGY = { strength_ratio = { tag = TUR ratio > 1 } }
					LBA = { strength_ratio = { tag = TUR ratio > 1 } }
					SUD = { strength_ratio = { tag = TUR ratio > 1 } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
